# Title: Mozilla Blocker - Block all Mozilla/Firefox Spying
# Description: No Mozilla version - This blocklist aims to block not just almost, but all traffic to "Mozilla: Not a Browser/Tech Company - A Political, AI, Data Collection and Advertisement Organization". Everything from mozilla is blocked including addons. Not recommended for regular users.
# Homepage: https://codeberg.org/privacyfilters/Mozilla-Blocker
# Source: https://codeberg.org/privacyfilters/Mozilla-Blocker/raw/branch/main/domains_nomozilla.txt
# License: https://codeberg.org/privacyfilters/Mozilla-Blocker/src/branch/main/LICENSE
# Expires: 1 day
# Last modified: 13 October 2026 15:38:43 UTC
# Version: 4.96
# Syntax: domains (including possible subdomains)
# Number of entries: 11089
#
# Not blocked: External urls that are used by other services
